# Skills · T · part 104

925 entries, timegravity-t02-gps-one-rate to timm. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **timegravity-t02-gps-one-rate**: Rebuild and verify the Time & Gravity T.02 instrument - a light clock whose tick stretches by the Lorentz factor, and the GPS daily satellite clock correction recomputed from GM, the orbit radius and c two independent ways with a GUM-style uncertainty budget - using a standard-library Python verifier with a planted-defect knockout. ([senseiewok/informatico.ai](https://github.com/senseiewok/informatico.ai/tree/HEAD/skills/timegravity/t02))
- **timegravity-t03-poisson-equivalence**: Rebuild and verify the Time & Gravity T.03 instrument - the weak-field Einstein equation reduced to Poisson's equation and solved on a radial grid for an Earth-mass sphere, plus two bodies of different composition released together (Eotvos parameter eta) - using a standard-library Python verifier whose planted defect is a composition-dependent fall. ([senseiewok/informatico.ai](https://github.com/senseiewok/informatico.ai/tree/HEAD/skills/timegravity/t03))
- **timegravity-t04-clock-visibility**: Rebuild and verify the Time & Gravity T.04 experiment description - a two-level clock in a superposition of two heights loses interferometric visibility V = |cos(omega dtau / 2)| as its proper times decohere (Zych et al. 2011) - with a standard-library Python verifier (closed form against explicit state overlap) whose planted defect is a clock that never decoheres. ([senseiewok/informatico.ai](https://github.com/senseiewok/informatico.ai/tree/HEAD/skills/timegravity/t04))
- **timegravity-t05-entanglement-channels**: Rebuild and verify the Time & Gravity T.05 instrument - the Bose-Marletto-Vedral four-branch entanglement phase (phi_ij = G m^2 t / hbar d_ij, concurrence |sin(delta phi / 2)|, Peres negativity under local dephasing) with three channels at equal weight (quantum, classical LOCC, disputed classical non-LOCC) - with a planted defect that lets a LOCC channel entangle. ([senseiewok/informatico.ai](https://github.com/senseiewok/informatico.ai/tree/HEAD/skills/timegravity/t05))
- **timegravity-t06-collapse-bound**: Rebuild and verify the Time & Gravity T.06 instrument - the Diosi-Penrose collapse proposal (tau = hbar / E_G for two displaced uniform spheres, closed form against numerical quadrature) and the Gran Sasso spontaneous-radiation bound on R0 recomputed from the published counts - with a planted defect that draws the bound on the wrong side. ([senseiewok/informatico.ai](https://github.com/senseiewok/informatico.ai/tree/HEAD/skills/timegravity/t06))
- **timegravity-t07-page-wootters**: Rebuild and verify the Time & Gravity T.07 toy - a Page-Wootters history state on a 16-level clock and a qubit that satisfies the timeless constraint H|Psi&gt;&gt; = 0 while conditioning on the clock recovers closed-form Schrodinger evolution; a pure-Python Jacobi eigensolver checks kernel membership - with a planted defect (a clock whose generator does not match the constraint). ([senseiewok/informatico.ai](https://github.com/senseiewok/informatico.ai/tree/HEAD/skills/timegravity/t07))
- **timegravity-t08-horizon-thermodynamics**: Rebuild and verify the Time & Gravity T.08 toy - a two-mode Rindler thermofield double on 16 Fock levels whose partial trace over the left wedge yields a Planck thermal density matrix on the right wedge (verified via a pure-Python Jacobi eigensolver), alongside Rindler proper-time redshift and Gauss-Legendre integration of dS = c^2 dM / T_H(M) = k_B c^3 A / (4 G hbar) - with a planted defect (r = 0 product state with zero cross-horizon entanglement). ([senseiewok/informatico.ai](https://github.com/senseiewok/informatico.ai/tree/HEAD/skills/timegravity/t08))
- **timegravity-t09-kerr-ringdown**: Rebuild and verify the Time & Gravity T.09 toy - Kerr horizon geometry, Smarr's identity, and Leaver's (1985) coupled angular and radial continued-fraction solver for the l=m=2, n=0 fundamental and n=1 first-overtone quasinormal modes across spin chi in [0, 0.90] - with a planted defect (spin-blind Schwarzschild area and spectrum on a spinning Kerr hole). ([senseiewok/informatico.ai](https://github.com/senseiewok/informatico.ai/tree/HEAD/skills/timegravity/t09))
- **timegravity-t10-page-curve**: Rebuild and verify the Time & Gravity T.10 toy - Page's counting argument on ten qubits: Haar-random pure states whose radiation entropy matches Page's closed form S(m,n) = sum_{k=n+1}^{mn} 1/k - (m-1)/(2n) at every cut, turns over at N/2 and falls back, with eigenvalues from a pure-Python Jacobi solver - whose planted defect is a non-unitary toy that follows Hawking's line and never turns over. ([senseiewok/informatico.ai](https://github.com/senseiewok/informatico.ai/tree/HEAD/skills/timegravity/t10))
- **timegravity-t11-einselection**: Rebuild and verify the Time & Gravity T.11 toy - a 1+6 qubit pure-dephasing pointer-state and Quantum Darwinism statevector model whose Jacobi-diagonalised partial traces match Zurek's closed-form mutual-information plateau I(S : E_k) = h_2(gamma^N) + h_2(gamma^k) - h_2(gamma^{N-k}) within 1e-12, paired with the Joos-Zeh and Romero-Isart R^6 T^9 dipole photon scattering localization table - with a planted defect (theta = 0 zero pointer imprinting). ([senseiewok/informatico.ai](https://github.com/senseiewok/informatico.ai/tree/HEAD/skills/timegravity/t11))
- **timegravity-t12-dark-clock**: Rebuild and verify the Time & Gravity T.12 toy - the Planck 2018 dark-to-baryonic matter budget (Omega_c / Omega_b = 5.3643), the gravitational proper-time step of an electromagnetically transparent flyby across a two-clock network (closed-form arsinh vs composite Gauss-Legendre quadrature within 1e-11), and the ultralight scalar clock modulation bound - with a planted defect (G_dark = 0 violating the Equivalence Principle). ([senseiewok/informatico.ai](https://github.com/senseiewok/informatico.ai/tree/HEAD/skills/timegravity/t12))
- **timegravity-t13-holonomy-soft**: Rebuild and verify the Time & Gravity T.13 capstone - Weinberg (1964, 1965) soft-photon, soft-graviton, and higher-spin S-matrix Ward identities, Christodoulou / Strominger-Zhiboedov gravitational-wave memory strain and arm holonomy vs Gauss-Legendre quadrature, one-loop chiral and mixed gauge-gravitational anomaly cancellation (N_c = 3), and the pure core Lean 4 Prelude companion HolonomySoftAnomaly.lean - with a planted-defect knockout (kappa_e != kappa_p and N_c = 2). ([senseiewok/informatico.ai](https://github.com/senseiewok/informatico.ai/tree/HEAD/skills/timegravity/t13))
